Third-party license and notice inventory

This file covers the full resolved dependency graphs, including non-Linux and test-only packages. Identical license texts are grouped by SHA-256. No third-party source is modified. External executables are not bundled. Unmodified MPL-2.0 crate sources are supplied as a separate release asset; source download URLs for all crates are recorded in dependency-licenses.json.
